P2955:  9282:           if(verdict == EMPTY_VALUE) continue;
P2956:  9283:           int v = (int)MathRound(verdict);
P2957:  9284:           if(v == 0) continue;
P2958:  9285:           divVal   = v;
P2959:  9286:           divKind  = (MathAbs(v) == 1) ? "regular" : "hidden";
P2960:  9287:           divOk    = (g_dir == DIR_LONG  && (v ==  1 || v ==  2)) ||
P2961:  9288:                      (g_dir == DIR_SHORT && (v == -1 || v == -2));
P2962:  9289:           break;
P2963:  9290:          }
P2964:  9291:       }
P2965:  9292:        //--- [P-EVICT-1] divergence-miss disposition: refused S4-origin holders
P2966:  9293:        //--- ABORT (DIV_FALLBACK); S3 pre-bind rollback kept; other origins keep

P2968:  9295:        //--- A6REFUSED debug-gated; STAND-DOWN fires when armed (wanted, alert-only).

P2971:  9298:         {

P2984:  9311:             //--- anchor/dir/session captured together for tuple atomicity; dir/session capture is harmless (no ResetSequence writes per S1(22) census); the suppression record must outlive the reset.

P3341:  9668:           //--- [P-SLDEF-2 E23] SLADDER: anchor-free rung census at S5 (print-
P3342:  9669:           //--- only; no selection, reference or verdict reads it). Enumerates
P3343:  9670:           //--- the protective-side swing buffer outward from the ENTRY bar
P3344:  9671:           //--- (this invocation's barShift), rungs 0..7. rungSlot = raw slot
P3345:  9672:           //--- distance from the entry bar (s - barShift, gaps included);
P3346:  9673:           //--- rungExt = index among rungs strictly more extreme (px) than
P3347:  9674:           //--- every rung inside (-1 otherwise; rung 0 is extreme by
P3348:  9675:           //--- definition, so rungExt is monotone by construction).
P3349:  9676:           //--- Protective-side test is against the row's live entry
P3350:  9677:           //--- (currentPrice), the same price rungR uses. imbCode 3 is
P3351:  9678:           //--- REPORTED, never applied: nothing is filtered, walked or
P3352:  9679:           //--- terminated here. rungR = |tp-entry|/|entry-px|, direction-
P3353:  9680:           //--- invariant magnitude; protective validity is NOT asserted.
P3354:  9681:           //--- distPts = (px - slRef) in points (SLIMBR sign convention).
P3355:  9682:           //--- isOBSwing: buffer-39 OB-swing-time read at the rung's own
P3356:  9683:           //--- eval shift equals the rung's price-bar time. isFracAnchor:
P3357:  9684:           //--- rung shift equals the post-guard fractal anchor; ladFresh=0
P3358:  9685:           //--- (stale walk shadows) forces 0. exceedsPrev vs the previous
P3359:  9686:           //--- RUNG: B = body strictly more extreme (body-through
P3360:  9687:           //--- equivalent), else W = wick strictly more extreme, else N;
P3361:  9688:           //--- rung 0 = N. Ties in nearest-rung search resolve inward.
P3362:  9689:           datetime ladBarT = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P3363:  9690:           int ladSwingBuf = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? FL_BUF_SWING_LOW : FL_BUF_SWING_HIGH;

P3365:  9692:           int ladFresh = slimbr_fresh ? 1 : 0;
P3366:  9693:           int ladGuardS = slimbr_fresh ? g_slimbr_fracGuardS : -1;
P3367:  9694:            double ladTpD = MathAbs(tpTarget - currentPrice);
P3368:  9695:            //--- [P-SLDEF-4 E31] rescope + derived window. refIsRung witnesses
P3369:  9696:            //--- the reference's own slot in the swing buffer (1 = occupied,
P3370:  9697:            //--- 0 = empty, -1 = slotless echo); refWalkSteps is the limb's
P3371:  9698:            //--- walked steps beside it (-1 = unattributable: stale or
P3372:  9699:            //--- slotless). Obligated = slot>=0 && isRung=1 && steps>0.
P3373:  9700:            //--- Zero-step OB extremes take REF_OB_DEEP below, never a
P3374:  9701:            //--- coverage failure; slotless echoes take nothing. A walk that
P3375:  9702:            //--- reached a non-swing (slot>=0 && isRung=0 && steps>0) halts
P3376:  9703:            //--- the ROW with operands (print-only refusal: the ladder, MATCH
P3377:  9704:            //--- and correspondence lines for that row are withheld; selection
P3378:  9705:            //--- and verdict are untouched).
P3379:  9706:            int wFHave = (slimbr_fresh && g_slimbr_fracClass != "UNRESOLVED") ? 1 : 0;

P3381:  9708:            int wBaseSlot = slimbr_fresh ? g_slimbr_obBaseS : -1;

P3398:  9725:            int wObT = (wTodaySlot >= 0 && wTodayIsRung == 1 && wTodaySteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3399:  9726:            int wObB = (wBaseSlot >= 0 && wBaseIsRung == 1 && wBaseSteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3400:  9727:            int wObN = (wNuanceSlot >= 0 && wNuanceIsRung == 1 && wNuanceSteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3401:  9728:            int wObF = (wFracSlot >= 0 && wFracIsRung == 1 && wFracSteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3402:  9729:            int wObFN = (wFracNuSlot >= 0 && wFracNuIsRung == 1 && wFracNuSteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3403:  9730:            int wObA = (wAnchorSlot >= 0 && wAnchorIsRung == 1 && wAnchorSteps > 0) ? 1 : 0;
P3404:  9731:            int wRowOk = 1;
P3405:  9732:            string wRowStatus = "OK";
P3406:  9733:            string wHaltName = "-"; int wHaltSlot = -1;
P3407:  9734:            if(wTodaySlot >= 0 && wTodayIsRung == 0 && wTodaySteps > 0) { wHaltName = "today"; wHaltSlot = wTodaySlot; }
P3408:  9735:            else if(wBaseSlot >= 0 && wBaseIsRung == 0 && wBaseSteps > 0) { wHaltName = "base"; wHaltSlot = wBaseSlot; }
P3409:  9736:            else if(wNuanceSlot >= 0 && wNuanceIsRung == 0 && wNuanceSteps > 0) { wHaltName = "nuance"; wHaltSlot = wNuanceSlot; }
P3410:  9737:            else if(wFracSlot >= 0 && wFracIsRung == 0 && wFracSteps > 0) { wHaltName = "frac"; wHaltSlot = wFracSlot; }
P3411:  9738:            else if(wFracNuSlot >= 0 && wFracNuIsRung == 0 && wFracNuSteps > 0) { wHaltName = "fracNu"; wHaltSlot = wFracNuSlot; }
P3412:  9739:            else if(wAnchorSlot >= 0 && wAnchorIsRung == 0 && wAnchorSteps > 0) { wHaltName = "anchor"; wHaltSlot = wAnchorSlot; }
P3413:  9740:            if(wHaltName != "-") { wRowOk = 0; wRowStatus = "HALT_E31_1"; }
P3414:  9741:            //--- derived window (E31.3): origin at the entry bar, span to the
P3415:  9742:            //--- deepest obligated slot plus the stated margin, floored at the
P3416:  9743:            //--- legacy 500 so non-extended rows enumerate byte-identical
P3417:  9744:            //--- ladders. Rung arrays hold rungs (never slots), so a wider
P3418:  9745:            //--- window costs iterations only.
P3419:  9746:            int wDeepest = -1;
P3420:  9747:            if(wObT == 1 && wTodaySlot > wDeepest) wDeepest = wTodaySlot;
P3421:  9748:            if(wObB == 1 && wBaseSlot > wDeepest) wDeepest = wBaseSlot;
P3422:  9749:            if(wObN == 1 && wNuanceSlot > wDeepest) wDeepest = wNuanceSlot;

P3424:  9751:            if(wObFN == 1 && wFracNuSlot > wDeepest) wDeepest = wFracNuSlot;
P3425:  9752:            if(wObA == 1 && wAnchorSlot > wDeepest) wDeepest = wAnchorSlot;
P3426:  9753:            int wNeed = wDeepest + SRJ_LAD_MARGIN_SLOTS;
P3427:  9754:            int wLimit = wNeed;
P3428:  9755:            if(wLimit < 500) wLimit = 500;
P3429:  9756:            int wLimitHit = 0;
P3430:  9757:            if(wNeed > SRJ_LAD_ABS_SLOT_CAP) { wLimit = SRJ_LAD_ABS_SLOT_CAP; wLimitHit = 1; }
P3431:  9758:            int wWalkOB0 = slimbr_fresh ? g_slimbr_startShift : -1;
P3432:  9759:            int wWalkFR0 = wFHave ? g_slimbr_fracGuardS : -1;
P3433:  9760:            //--- E31.5: an obligated slot past the effective window halts the
P3434:  9761:            //--- row with both windows (reachable only when truncated: the
P3435:  9762:            //--- derived span covers every obligated slot by construction).
P3436:  9763:            //--- "The walk read" = the walk's produced reference slots, not
P3437:  9764:            //--- every scanned slot (the walk's 500-span legitimately exceeds
P3438:  9765:            //--- any ladder window on unobligated ground).
P3439:  9766:            if(wRowOk == 1)
P3440:  9767:              {
P3441:  9768:               int wStranded = -1;
P3442:  9769:               if(wObT == 1 && wTodaySlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wTodaySlot;
P3443:  9770:               else if(wObB == 1 && wBaseSlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wBaseSlot;
P3444:  9771:               else if(wObN == 1 && wNuanceSlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wNuanceSlot;
P3445:  9772:               else if(wObF == 1 && wFracSlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wFracSlot;
P3446:  9773:               else if(wObFN == 1 && wFracNuSlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wFracNuSlot;
P3447:  9774:               else if(wObA == 1 && wAnchorSlot > barShift + wLimit) wStranded = wAnchorSlot;
P3448:  9775:               if(wStranded >= 0) { wRowOk = 0; wRowStatus = "HALT_E31_5"; wHaltSlot = wStranded; }
P3449:  9776:              }
P3450:  9777:            //--- [P-SLDEF-4 E32] decision-row claim for this S5 row (labels
P3451:  9778:            //--- only; the firing flag is set at the PASS site below).
P3452:  9779:            int ladDecIdx = -1;
P3453:  9780:            int ladETaken[3]; ladETaken[0] = 0; ladETaken[1] = 0; ladETaken[2] = 0;
P3454:  9781:            if(wRowOk == 1 && g_dec_n < SRJ_DEC_MAXROWS)
P3455:  9782:              {
P3456:  9783:               ladDecIdx = g_dec_n; g_dec_n++;
P3457:  9784:               g_dec_barT[ladDecIdx] = ladBarT;
P3458:  9785:               g_dec_dir[ladDecIdx] = (g_dir == DIR_LONG) ? 1 : -1;
P3459:  9786:               g_dec_covers[ladDecIdx] = 0;
P3460:  9787:               g_dec_rungs[ladDecIdx] = 0;
P3461:  9788:               g_dec_fired[ladDecIdx] = 0;
P3462:  9789:               g_dec_sHave[ladDecIdx] = 0; g_dec_eHave[ladDecIdx] = 0;
P3463:  9790:               g_dec_mStatus[ladDecIdx] = "NOLEVEL_FILED";
P3464:  9791:               g_dec_mLevel[ladDecIdx] = 0.0; g_dec_mRung[ladDecIdx] = -1;
P3465:  9792:               g_dec_mSlot[ladDecIdx] = -1; g_dec_mExt[ladDecIdx] = -1;
P3466:  9793:               g_dec_mResid[ladDecIdx] = 0; g_dec_mT[ladDecIdx] = "-";
P3467:  9794:               g_dec_mPx[ladDecIdx] = 0.0; g_dec_mR[ladDecIdx] = 0.0;
P3468:  9795:              }
P3469:  9796:            //--- [P-SLDEF-3 E27] coverage bound replaces the rung count: the
P3470:  9797:           //--- ladder runs until its deepest rung sits strictly beyond every
P3471:  9798:           //--- genuine reference printed for the row (values, protective
P3472:  9799:           //--- side), or the hard slot bound stops it. Rung count is an
P3473:  9800:           //--- output. Arrays sized past the slot bound (500 slots max).
P3474:  9801:            int ladFHave = (slimbr_fresh && g_slimbr_fracClass != "UNRESOLVED") ? 1 : 0;
P3475:  9802:            //--- [P-SLDEF-4 E31] cover target over the rung-obligated subset
P3476:  9803:            //--- only (rescope): the most protective obligated value. An empty
P3477:  9804:            //--- obligated set (zero-step rows such as 9/08) covers vacuously
P3478:  9805:            //--- (ladCovered seeds 1 with no target below).
P3479:  9806:            double wCoverV[6]; int wCoverN = 0;
P3480:  9807:            if(wObT == 1) { wCoverV[wCoverN] = slRef; wCoverN++; }
P3481:  9808:            if(wObB == 1) { wCoverV[wCoverN] = g_slimbr_base; wCoverN++; }
P3482:  9809:            if(wObN == 1) { wCoverV[wCoverN] = g_slimbr_nuance; wCoverN++; }

P3485:  9812:            if(wObA == 1) { wCoverV[wCoverN] = g_slimbr_frRetV; wCoverN++; }
P3486:  9813:            //--- [P-SLDEF-5 E35/gate 9] ext1 joins the cover target (obligated
P3487:  9814:            //--- by construction when defined); ladObligN counts the six walk
P3488:  9815:            //--- refs (rider sense), so an empty six-set still names VACUOUS.
P3489:  9816:            int wCoverX1 = (e35_def == 1) ? 1 : 0;
P3490:  9817:            int wCoverTot = wCoverN + wCoverX1;
P3491:  9818:            double ladCoverT = 0.0;
P3492:  9819:            if(wCoverTot > 0)
P3493:  9820:              {
P3494:  9821:               ladCoverT = (wCoverX1 == 1) ? e35_px : wCoverV[0];

P4423: 10857: //====================== [P-NEWS-1 E20/E21/E22] news-blackout census =================
P4424: 10858: //--- Print-only. No verdict moves, no selection changes, MTEXIT untouched.
P4425: 10859: //--- E20 pinned table: 11 {eventTimeET, kind} rows transcribed from
P4426: 10860: //--- DRAFT_NEWS-EVENTS-2026.csv @ SHA256 5FFF5C76...EF1F134. No broker-time
P4427: 10861: //--- field, no stored offset: ET wall -> server at read time through
P4428: 10862: //--- TC_ZoneToServer(..., TZ_NEWYORK), the same converter the session
P4429: 10863: //--- windows use (US + server DST rules inside TickCore, evaluated on the
P4430: 10864: //--- naive wall timestamp; event hours 08:30/14:00 ET never touch the
P4431: 10865: //--- 02:00 transition hours, so the documented boundary-hour bound cannot
P4432: 10866: //--- engage these rows). Accessors are switch-based: no init order, no
P4433: 10867: //--- global-constructor dependence.
P4434: 10868: //--- E21: SrjInNewsBlackout is THE predicate body. The census calls it now;
P4435: 10869: //--- the exit side and the entry side will call the same body later. There
P4436: 10870: //--- is no second implementation (E21 halt-1 satisfied by construction).
P4437: 10871: //--- Window [newsBarOpen - 1*PS, newsBarOpen + 2*PS); newsBar containment
P4438: 10872: //--- [barOpen, barOpen + PS). No literal minutes: the shape is PS multiples;
P4439: 10873: //--- the /60 in offsetMinutes is a seconds-to-minutes unit, not a window term.
P4440: 10874: #define SRJ_NEWS_ROWS 11
P4441: 10875: #define SHADOW_NEWS true
P4442: 10876: //--- Pilot range (server frame) for inWindow membership. From RECON1_P1 per
P4443: 10877: //--- the packet's "full window" term — NOT derived from Bars()/iTime: those
P4444: 10878: //--- return the full broker history depth (years), and iTime(0) at lazy-init
P4445: 10879: //--- is the first bar, so data-derived bounds exclude every later window
P4446: 10880: //--- (RECON12 measured rowsInWindow=0 on exactly this defect). If the packet
P4447: 10881: //--- ever moves window, these two lines move with it.
P4448: 10882: #define SRJ_PILOT_FROM D'2026.08.26 00:00'
P4449: 10883: #define SRJ_PILOT_TO   D'2026.09.10 00:00'
P4450: 10884: string SrjNewsET(const int i)
P4451: 10885:    {
P4452: 10886:     switch(i)
P4453: 10887:       {
P4454: 10888:        case  0: return "2026-09-04 08:30";
P4455: 10889:        case  1: return "2026-09-11 08:30";
P4456: 10890:        case  2: return "2026-09-16 14:00";
P4457: 10891:        case  3: return "2026-10-02 08:30";
P4458: 10892:        case  4: return "2026-10-14 08:30";
P4459: 10893:        case  5: return "2026-10-28 14:00";
P4460: 10894:        case  6: return "2026-11-06 08:30";
P4461: 10895:        case  7: return "2026-11-10 08:30";
P4462: 10896:        case  8: return "2026-12-04 08:30";
P4463: 10897:        case  9: return "2026-12-09 14:00";
P4464: 10898:        case 10: return "2026-12-10 08:30";
P4465: 10899:       }
P4466: 10900:     return "";
P4467: 10901:    }
P4468: 10902: string SrjNewsKind(const int i)
P4469: 10903:    {
P4470: 10904:     switch(i)
P4471: 10905:       {
P4472: 10906:        case  0: return "NFP";
P4473: 10907:        case  1: return "CPI";
P4474: 10908:        case  2: return "FOMC";
P4475: 10909:        case  3: return "NFP";
P4476: 10910:        case  4: return "CPI";
P4477: 10911:        case  5: return "FOMC";
P4478: 10912:        case  6: return "NFP";
P4479: 10913:        case  7: return "CPI";
P4480: 10914:        case  8: return "NFP";
P4481: 10915:        case  9: return "FOMC";
P4482: 10916:        case 10: return "CPI";
P4483: 10917:       }
P4484: 10918:     return "";
P4485: 10919:    }
P4486: 10920: //--- "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM" -> naive wall datetime. StringToTime is server-frame
P4487: 10921: //--- so the pinned format is parsed manually; the result is NEVER passed to
P4488: 10922: //--- iTime/CopyBuffer without TC_ZoneToServer first.
P4489: 10923: datetime SrjNewsEtWall(const int i)
P4490: 10924:    {
P4491: 10925:     string s = SrjNewsET(i);
P4492: 10926:     int y  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 0, 4);
P4493: 10927:     int mo = (int)StringSubstr(s, 5, 2);
P4494: 10928:     int d  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 8, 2);
P4495: 10929:     int h  = (int)StringSubstr(s, 11, 2);
P4496: 10930:     int mi = (int)StringSubstr(s, 14, 2);
P4497: 10931:     return TC_MakeTime(y, mo, d, h, mi);
P4498: 10932:    }
P4499: 10933: datetime g_news_newsBar[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4500: 10934: datetime g_news_winS[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4501: 10935: datetime g_news_winE[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4502: 10936: int      g_news_offMin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4503: 10937: int      g_news_inWin[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4504: 10938: int      g_news_halted[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4505: 10939: int      g_news_rowSnap[SRJ_NEWS_ROWS];
P4506: 10940: int      g_news_nWin = 0;
P4507: 10941: int      g_news_overlaps = 0;
P4508: 10942: string   g_news_overlapList = "";
P4509: 10943: bool     g_news_init = false;
P4510: 10944: int      g_news_memberBars = 0;
P4511: 10945: int      g_news_slimbInWin = 0;
P4512: 10946: int      g_news_s5InWin = 0;
P4513: 10947: int      g_news_flatNews = 0;
P4514: 10948: int      g_news_flatDay = 0;
P4515: 10949: int      g_news_flatWeek = 0;
P4516: 10950: datetime g_news_dayMarks[32];
P4517: 10951: int      g_news_dayDone[32];
P4518: 10952: int      g_news_dayN = 0;
P4519: 10953: string   g_news_friET = "";
P4520: 10954: datetime g_news_friMarks[8];
P4521: 10955: int      g_news_friDone[8];
P4522: 10956: int      g_news_friN = 0;
P4523: 10957: bool SrjInNewsBlackout(const datetime barOpen, string &kindOut, int &posOut, int &rowOut)
P4524: 10958:    {
P4525: 10959:     kindOut = "-"; posOut = -1; rowOut = -1;
P4526: 10960:     if(!g_news_init) return false;
P4527: 10961:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4528: 10962:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4529: 10963:       {
P4530: 10964:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_halted[i] == 1) continue;
P4531: 10965:        if(barOpen < g_news_winS[i] || barOpen >= g_news_winE[i]) continue;
P4532: 10966:        kindOut = SrjNewsKind(i); rowOut = i;
P4533: 10967:        if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i]) posOut = 0;
P4534: 10968:        else if(barOpen < g_news_newsBar[i] + ps) posOut = 1;
P4535: 10969:        else posOut = 2;
P4536: 10970:        return true;
P4537: 10971:       }
P4538: 10972:     return false;
P4539: 10973:    }
P4540: 10974: //--- Lazy init on the first closed bar. Conversions need no history; the E21
P4541: 10975: //--- gap check does NOT run here (tester series only extend to the current
P4542: 10976: //--- bar — September bars don't exist yet on the first August bar, and a
P4543: 10977: //--- check now would false-halt; RECON12b measured exactly that). Pilot
P4544: 10978: //--- range is SRJ_PILOT_FROM/TO.
P4545: 10979: void SrjNewsInit()
P4546: 10980:    {
P4547: 10981:     if(g_news_init) return;
P4548: 10982:     g_news_init = true;
P4549: 10983:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4550: 10984:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4551: 10985:       {
P4552: 10986:        datetime wall = SrjNewsEtWall(i);
P4553: 10987:        datetime srv  = TC_ZoneToServer(wall, TZ_NEWYORK);
P4554: 10988:        g_news_offMin[i] = (int)(((long)srv - (long)wall) / 60);
P4555: 10989:        datetime nb = (datetime)(((long)srv / ps) * ps);
P4556: 10990:        g_news_newsBar[i] = nb;
P4557: 10991:        g_news_winS[i] = (datetime)((long)nb - ps);
P4558: 10992:        g_news_winE[i] = (datetime)((long)nb + 2 * ps);
P4559: 10993:        g_news_inWin[i] = (g_news_winE[i] > SRJ_PILOT_FROM && g_news_winS[i] < SRJ_PILOT_TO) ? 1 : 0;
P4560: 10994:        g_news_halted[i] = 0;
P4561: 10995:        g_news_rowSnap[i] = 0;
P4562: 10996:       }
P4563: 10997:     //--- Day marks: 16:55 ET (= 17:00 daily close minus 5 min, the dayFlat
P4564: 10998:     //--- census definition, quoted in BLACKOUT_CENSUS) per calendar date in
P4565: 10999:     //--- range; Friday marks: 17:00 ET (the weekFlat census definition). Both
P4566: 11000:     //--- resolved through the same converter. Noon-dow is zone-safe: at
P4567: 11001:     //--- midday the ET and server dates always agree.
P4568: 11002:     g_news_dayN = 0;
P4569: 11003:     g_news_friN = 0;
P4570: 11004:     g_news_friET = "";
P4571: 11005:     datetime cur = TC_DayStart(SRJ_PILOT_FROM);
P4572: 11006:     while(cur < SRJ_PILOT_TO && g_news_dayN < 32)
P4573: 11007:       {
P4574: 11008:        MqlDateTime dd; TimeToStruct(cur, dd);
P4575: 11009:        g_news_dayMarks[g_news_dayN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 16, 55), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4576: 11010:        g_news_dayDone[g_news_dayN] = 0;
P4577: 11011:        g_news_dayN++;
P4578: 11012:        MqlDateTime noon; TimeToStruct(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 12, 0), noon);
P4579: 11013:        if(noon.day_of_week == 5 && g_news_friN < 8)
P4580: 11014:          {
P4581: 11015:           g_news_friMarks[g_news_friN] = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day, 17, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4582: 11016:           g_news_friDone[g_news_friN] = 0;
P4583: 11017:           g_news_friN++;
P4584: 11018:           string one = StringFormat("%04d-%02d-%02d 17:00", dd.year, dd.mon, dd.day);
P4585: 11019:           g_news_friET += ((g_news_friET == "") ? "" : "|") + one;
P4586: 11020:          }
P4587: 11021:        cur = TC_ShiftDayStart(cur, 1);
P4588: 11022:       }
P4589: 11023:    }
P4590: 11024: //--- "Open" for the flat census. g_mtrade.active is STICKY: it is set at
P4591: 11025: //--- fill and cleared only by the next fill's MtReset, so a closed trade
P4592: 11026: //--- still reads active. state goes MT_CLOSED at close (normal path). Both
P4593: 11027: //--- conditions together are the true open interval (RECON12b overcounted
P4594: 11028: //--- dayFlat 12 and weekFlat 2 on the sticky flag alone).
P4595: 11029: bool SrjNewsIsOpen()
P4596: 11030:    {
P4597: 11031:     return (g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state != MT_CLOSED);
P4598: 11032:    }
P4599: 11033: //--- End-of-run row finalization. Full history exists now, so the E21 gap
P4600: 11034: //--- check is sound: the event instant must sit inside a real bar (exact
P4601: 11035: //--- containment, never snapped). Halted rows print HALT plus their ROW
P4602: 11036: //--- with inWindow=0 and are excluded from every expectation.
P4603: 11037: void SrjNewsFinalize()
P4604: 11038:    {
P4605: 11039:     long ps = (long)PeriodSeconds(PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4606: 11040:     g_news_nWin = 0;
P4607: 11041:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4608: 11042:       {
P4609: 11043:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 1)
P4610: 11044:          {
P4611: 11045:           datetime srv = TC_ZoneToServer(SrjNewsEtWall(i), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4612: 11046:           if(iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, srv, true) < 0)
P4613: 11047:             {
P4614: 11048:              g_news_halted[i] = 1; g_news_inWin[i] = 0;
P4615: 11049:              string haltLine = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_HALT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s reason=GAP_NO_BAR",
P4616: 11050:                                             SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i));
P4617: 11051:              LwAudit("BLACKOUT_HALT", haltLine);
P4618: 11052:              Print(haltLine);
P4619: 11053:             }
P4620: 11054:           else
P4621: 11055:              g_news_nWin++;
P4622: 11056:          }
P4623: 11057:        int spanned = (int)(((long)g_news_winE[i] - (long)g_news_winS[i]) / ps);
P4624: 11058:        string rl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_ROW kind=%s eventTimeET=%s newsBarOpen=%s windowStart=%s windowEnd=%s offsetMinutes=%d inWindow=%d barsSpanned=%d",
P4625: 11059:                                 SrjNewsKind(i), SrjNewsET(i),
P4626: 11060:                                 TimeToString(g_news_newsBar[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4627: 11061:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winS[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4628: 11062:                                 TimeToString(g_news_winE[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4629: 11063:                                 g_news_offMin[i], g_news_inWin[i], spanned);
P4630: 11064:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_ROW", rl);
P4631: 11065:        Print(rl);
P4632: 11066:       }
P4633: 11067:     for(int a = 0; a < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; a++)
P4634: 11068:         for(int b = a + 1; b < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; b++)
P4635: 11069:            if(g_news_inWin[a] == 1 && g_news_inWin[b] == 1 &&
P4636: 11070:               g_news_winS[a] < g_news_winE[b] && g_news_winS[b] < g_news_winE[a])
P4637: 11071:              { g_news_overlaps++; g_news_overlapList += ((g_news_overlaps > 1) ? "|" : "") + SrjNewsKind(a) + "@" + SrjNewsET(a) + "x" + SrjNewsKind(b) + "@" + SrjNewsET(b); }
P4638: 11072:    }
P4639: 11073: //--- Per-closed-bar census hook. Read-only: counts and prints only. Called at
P4640: 11074: //--- the END of OnTick so g_mtrade reflects the settled post-eval state.
P4641: 11075: void SrjNewsOnBar(const datetime barTime)
P4642: 11076:    {
P4643: 11077:     string k; int pos, row;
P4644: 11078:     if(SrjInNewsBlackout(barTime, k, pos, row))
P4645: 11079:       {
P4646: 11080:        g_news_memberBars++;
P4647: 11081:        string bl = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] BLACKOUT_BAR barTime=%s kind=%s pos=%s",
P4648: 11082:                                 TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), k,
P4649: 11083:                                 (pos == 0) ? "PRE" : ((pos == 1) ? "NEWS" : "POST"));
P4650: 11084:        LwAudit("BLACKOUT_BAR", bl);
P4651: 11085:        Print(bl);
P4652: 11086:       }
P4653: 11087:     //--- Flat populations, edge-triggered, once each. "Open" = SrjNewsIsOpen().
P4654: 11088:     for(int i = 0; i < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; i++)
P4655: 11089:       {
P4656: 11090:        if(g_news_inWin[i] == 0 || g_news_rowSnap[i] == 1) continue;
P4657: 11091:        if(barTime >= g_news_winS[i])
P4658: 11092:          { g_news_rowSnap[i] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatNews++; }
P4659: 11093:       }
P4660: 11094:     for(int d = 0; d < g_news_dayN; d++)
P4661: 11095:       {
P4662: 11096:        if(g_news_dayDone[d] == 1) continue;
P4663: 11097:        if(barTime >= g_news_dayMarks[d])
P4664: 11098:          { g_news_dayDone[d] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatDay++; }
P4665: 11099:       }
P4666: 11100:     for(int f = 0; f < g_news_friN; f++)
P4667: 11101:       {
P4668: 11102:        if(g_news_friDone[f] == 1) continue;
P4669: 11103:        if(barTime >= g_news_friMarks[f])
P4670: 11104:          { g_news_friDone[f] = 1; if(SrjNewsIsOpen()) g_news_flatWeek++; }
P4671: 11105:       }
P4672: 11106:    }
P4673: 11107: 
P4674: 11108: //====================== OnInit =========================================
P4675: 11109: int OnInit()
P4676: 11110:   {
P4677: 11111:    InitAuthorityTable();
P4678: 11112: 
P4679: 11113:    if(!(bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER)) TC_DetectServerOffset();
P4680: 11114:    TC_DetectServerOffsetNow();
P4681: 11115: 
P4682: 11116:    {
P4683: 11117:     datetime srvNow   = TimeCurrent();
P4684: 11118:     datetime gmtNow   = TimeGMT();
P4685: 11119:     bool     inTester = (bool)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER);
P4686: 11120: 
P4687: 11121:     MqlDateTime d;
P4688: 11122:     TimeToStruct(TC_DayStart(srvNow), d);
P4689: 11123: 
P4690: 11124:     datetime lonFromSrv = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  2, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4691: 11125:     datetime lonToSrv   = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  5, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4692: 11126:     datetime nyFromSrv  = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day,  7, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4693: 11127:     datetime nyToSrv    = TC_ZoneToServer(TC_MakeTime(d.year, d.mon, d.day, 12, 0, 0), TZ_NEWYORK);
P4694: 11128: 
P4695: 11129:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] TimeCurrent=%s TimeGMT=%s srvMinusGmt=%+ds "
P4696: 11130:                 "gtc_serverGmtBase=%+ds offsetNow=%s tester=%s",
P4697: 11131:                 TimeToString(srvNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4698: 11132:                 TimeToString(gmtNow, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS),
P4699: 11133:                 (int)((long)srvNow - (long)gmtNow),
P4700: 11134:                 gtc_serverGmtBase,
P4701: 11135:                 TC_ServerOffsetText(),
P4702: 11136:                 (inTester ? "YES" : "NO"));
P4703: 11137: 
P4704: 11138:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] session windows resolved to SERVER frame from ET date "
P4705: 11139:                 "%04d.%02d.%02d:  LONDON 02:00-05:00 ET = %s .. %s   |   "
P4706: 11140:                 "NYAM 07:00-12:00 ET = %s .. %s",
P4707: 11141:                 d.year, d.mon, d.day,
P4708: 11142:                 TimeToString(lonFromSrv, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4709: 11143:                 TimeToString(lonToSrv,   TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4710: 11144:                 TimeToString(nyFromSrv,  TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
P4711: 11145:                 TimeToString(nyToSrv,    TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
P4712: 11146: 
P4713: 11147:     if(srvNow <= 0 || gmtNow <= 0)
P4714: 11148:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TimeCurrent() or TimeGMT() returned <= 0...");
P4715: 11149: 
P4716: 11150:     if(inTester && srvNow == gmtNow)
P4717: 11151:        Print("[SRJ-EA][CLOCK] WARNING: TESTER RUN with TimeGMT()==TimeCurrent()...");
P4718: 11152:    }
P4719: 11153: 
P4720: 11154:    ResetLastError();
P4721: 11155:    g_hPoi = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpPoiMarkerName,
P4722: 11156:                     InpPoi_UseSeed, InpPoi_BinPips, InpPoi_WeightMode);
P4723: 11157:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] POI handle=%d err=%d", g_hPoi, GetLastError());
P4724: 11158:    ResetLastError();
P4725: 11159:    g_hCqd = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpCqdName,
P4726: 11160:                     PERIOD_D1, InpCqd_NoReset, true,
P4727: 11161:                     InpCqd_MaxCarryBars, InpCqd_MaxBackfillDays);
P4728: 11162:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] CQD handle=%d err=%d", g_hCqd, GetLastError());
P4729: 11163:    ResetLastError();
P4730: 11164:    g_hFlow = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpFlowLogicName,
P4731: 11165:                      1, InpFL_HtfLookbackBars,
P4732: 11166:                      //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE1] confirmed selection (F252
P4733: 11167:                      //--- inUseConfirmedHTFOnly; EU preservation sibling row
P4734: 11168:                      //--- grades the global effect).
P4735: 11169:                      PERIOD_H4, PERIOD_H1, PERIOD_M15, true, 60);
P4736: 11170:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] Flow handle=%d err=%d", g_hFlow, GetLastError());
P4737: 11171:     if(g_hPoi == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hCqd == INVALID_HANDLE || g_hFlow == INVALID_HANDLE)
P4738: 11172:       { Print("[SRJ-EA] OnInit FAILED: one or more iCustom handles are invalid."); return INIT_FAILED; }
P4739: 11173:     //--- [P-SEL-1 E51] platform-Fractals handles (own pair; NEVER in the
P4740: 11174:     //--- run-blocking conjunction above - a SEL handle failure reports
P4741: 11175:     //--- SELHALT end-of-run, it never stops the baseline run).
P4742: 11176:     ResetLastError();
P4743: 11177:     g_selfracM5 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT);
P4744: 11178:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle M5=%d err=%d", g_selfracM5, GetLastError());
P4745: 11179:     ResetLastError();
P4746: 11180:     g_selfracH1 = iFractals(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1);
P4747: 11181:     PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] SEL fractal handle H1=%d err=%d", g_selfracH1, GetLastError());
P4748: 11182:     SrjSideNote("INIT", g_dir);
P4749: 11183:     ResetSequence();
P4750: 11184:     //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E13 + amendment, P-SLDEF-4 E34] FRAME_NOTE, once per run:
P4751: 11185:     //--- the six conventions a later session could silently invert. (1) Slot
P4752: 11186:     //--- frame: every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4753: 11187:    //--- every printed shift is ReadFlow frame, CopyBuffer position =
P4754: 11188:    //--- eval shift + FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET (the settled slot). (2) Apex frame: a
P4755: 11189:    //--- swing value at eval shift s is priced at ApexShift(s) = s + offset;
P4756: 11190:    //--- barTime(s) prints iTime at that price shift - the code's own belief.
P4757: 11191:    //--- (3) Protective sign: raw deltaPts is (ref - today) in points, so
P4758: 11192:    //--- LONG-protective prints negative and SHORT-protective positive;
P4759: 11193:    //--- outwardPts = LONG ? -delta : +delta normalizes to outward-positive.
P4760: 11194:    //--- (4) Population identity [P-NEWS-1 verdict rule]: every gate count
P4761: 11195:    //--- names its population in the same sentence - input-side (guard
P4762: 11196:    //--- applications, raw encounters) vs result-side (post-walk values).
P4763: 11197:     //--- Labels (bar=, barTime) are server time via iTime/TimeToString.
P4764: 11198:     //--- (6) Read windows per reader [P-SLDEF-4 E31]: the ladder reads from
P4765: 11199:     //--- the entry bar to the deepest rung-obligated slot plus margin
P4766: 11200:     //--- (SLADWIN ladWindowStart/ladWindowSpan/ladReadLimit); the OB walk
P4767: 11201:     //--- reads walkStart..walkStart+500, the fractal walk guardStart..
P4768: 11202:     //--- guardStart+500 (SLADWIN walkWinOB/walkWinFR). A ladder window and a
P4769: 11203:     //--- walk window are DIFFERENT windows: coverage is by slot reach, and
P4770: 11204:     //--- ladLimitHit rows are UNCOVERED_READ_LIMIT, never silent.
P4771: 11205:     //--- (7) Origin per site [P-ADOPT-1 E48]: S5 = strict next-open
P4772: 11206:     //--- (caller-stamped entry bar; a stale stamp halts the row, never
P4773: 11207:     //--- substitutes); S2POLL/S3ARM = eval-bar close (the shared
P4774: 11208:     //--- memo-path convention both sites' live R already uses).
P4775: 11209:     //--- THRESHOLD: the live minimum-R is an artifact of the run. Source is
P4776: 11210:    //--- "ini" when the value differs from the compiled default (an ini-set
P4777: 11211:    //--- 1.0 is indistinguishable - recorded as compiled_default).
P4778: 11212:      string frame_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] FRAME_NOTE offset=%d apex=s+%d labels=server-time "
P4779: 11213:                  "protectiveSign=LONG-lower/SHORT-higher outward=LONG(-d)/SHORT(+d) "
P4780: 11214:                  "populations=inputVsResultNamed ladObligN=6refs ladCovers=oblig+ext1 "

P4782: 11216:                  "readwin=ladder:entryBar+span|walkOB:walkStart+500|walkFR:guardStart+500 "
P4783: 11217:                  "origin=S5:nextOpen-strictHalt|S2POLL+S3ARM:evalClose "
P4784: 11218:                  "THRESHOLD minRewardRisk=%.2f source=%s",
P4785: 11219:                FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, FLOW_SHIFT_OFFSET, InpMinRewardRisk,
P4786: 11220:                ((InpMinRewardRisk == 1.0) ? "compiled_default" : "ini"));
P4787: 11221:    LwAudit("FRAME_NOTE", frame_note);
P4788: 11222:    Print(frame_note);
P4789: 11223:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E20] pinned-table note, once per run. Digest is a source
P4790: 11224:    //--- string constant (the human check); the BLACKOUT_ROW emissions at the
P4791: 11225:    //--- first bar are the audit artifact. No history needed here.
P4792: 11226:    string table_note = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] TABLE_NOTE rows=%d digest=%s anchor=%s",
P4793: 11227:                                     SRJ_NEWS_ROWS,
P4794: 11228:                                     "5FFF5C762DABCAB7811C598D8B16942BBCBD5F9D94C91D0BB54CF8C36EF1F134",
P4795: 11229:                                     "21:00_broker");
P4796: 11230:     LwAudit("TABLE_NOTE", table_note);
P4797: 11231:     Print(table_note);
P4798: 11232:     //--- [P-ORIGIN-1 §2/FREEZE] site-origin manifest, once per run.
P4799: 11233:     //--- Candidate bindings resolve ONLY at the seven frozen example
P4800: 11234:     //--- bars (SrjOriginEntry); UNBOUND elsewhere — no fallback, and
P4801: 11235:     //--- diagnostic rows exist only at example bars by construction.
P4802: 11236:     //--- Per-example bindings + rule declaration live in
P4803: 11237:     //--- BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md (frozen pre-execution).
P4804: 11238:     string sl60_m1 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S5-direct role=compute existingBinding=stamped-strict-next-open candidateRule=frozen-HAND-entry-at-R1-R5-else-UNBOUND memoRel=none freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4805: 11239:     string sl60_m2 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S2POLL role=compute+memo-write existingBinding=eval-bar-close candidateRule=frozen-HAND-entry-at-T1-T2-else-UNBOUND memoRel=writes-gen-stamped freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4806: 11240:     string sl60_m3 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=S3ARM role=compute+memo-write+read existingBinding=eval-bar-close candidateRule=UNBOUND-no-example-bars memoRel=writes-on-COMPUTE-reads-on-HIT freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4807: 11241:     string sl60_m4 = "[SRJ-EA] ORIGIN_MANIFEST fields=6 site=memo-read role=read existingBinding=HIT-returns-stored candidateRule=not-applicable memoRel=HIT-reports-req-vs-stored-plus-genID freeze=BUILDER_FREEZE_PORIGIN1.md";
P4808: 11242:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m1); Print(sl60_m1);
P4809: 11243:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m2); Print(sl60_m2);
P4810: 11244:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m3); Print(sl60_m3);
P4811: 11245:     LwAudit("ORIGIN_MANIFEST", sl60_m4); Print(sl60_m4);
P4812: 11246:     Print("[SRJ-EA] Initialised.");
P4813: 11247:    return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
P4814: 11248:   }

P4885: 11319:    if(InpDebugLog)
P4886: 11320:      {
P4887: 11321:       //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E14] N1 equality tally. Counters only; no branch reads

P4889: 11323:       //--- DetectPoiRetest (`l <= L-P+EPS && bodyLo >= L-EPS`, SHORT mirror),

P4905: 11339:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1b E15] width audit, once per run per class. truncated

P5018: 11452:           LwAudit("SLADDER_DECISION_ROW", decRow);

P5020: 11454:           if(g_dec_fired[decR] == 1)
P5021: 11455:             {
P5022: 11456:              for(int decK = 0; decK < g_dec_sHave[decR]; decK++)
P5023: 11457:                {

P5034: 11468:                }

P5036: 11470:                {

P5050: 11484:           //--- [P-SLDEF-6 E43] memo-probe FINAL (ungated): S5 evaluations,

P5073: 11507:                       g_sl48_n, g_sl48_dis, g_sl48_nS5, g_sl48_disS5, g_sl48_n2P, g_sl48_dis2P, g_sl48_n3A, g_sl48_dis3A, g_sl48_altNA);
P5074: 11508:           LwAudit("SLORIG48_FINAL", sl48_fin);

P5131: 11565: //====================== [P-EXITMODEL] exit-phase helpers =============================
P5132: 11566: //--- The section 5.1 body-close trigger set, from the compile-time MT_EXIT_SCOPE,
P5133: 11567: //--- classified through the ruled hierarchy (charter 9.1: AVP-POC over VWAP inside
P5134: 11568: //--- each family; the origin/anchor line's own break always exits - 9.1(2)).
P5135: 11569: bool MtIsBreakTrigger(const int k)
P5136: 11570:   {
P5137: 11571:    if(k == g_mtrade.anchorLine) return true;   // 9.1(2): the origin entry POI
P5138: 11572:    if(MT_EXIT_SCOPE == MT_SCOPE_ALL) return true;
P5139: 11573:    if(MT_EXIT_SCOPE == MT_SCOPE_ANCHOR) return false;
P5140: 11574:    return ((k % 2) == 0);   // FAMILY_POC: the POC buffers are the even indices
P5141: 11575:   }
P5142: 11576: 
P5143: 11577: //--- The TP scan replicates ComputeNearestTpTarget's admission EXACTLY but takes the
P5144: 11578: //--- anchor tier from the TRADE's record: the shared function reads the working-set
P5145: 11579: //--- g_anchorLine, which ResetSequence wipes at the signal, and the exit phase runs
P5146: 11580: //--- post-reset. DECLARED DUPLICATION: the entry pipeline is byte-untouched (spec
P5147: 11581: //--- section 7). TpTargetUpdateBest's zone guard (Task 31/Ruling 7c) reads the
P5148: 11582: //--- working-set zone globals, which are 0.0 post-reset, so the guard is inert here -
P5149: 11583: //--- consistent with section 2.2 ("a touch, no geometry") and section 5.3 (no
P5150: 11584: //--- distance/size/width in the exit rules). Q6 ruling honored: re-computed per bar,
P5151: 11585: //--- the nearest valid target, even if less than 1R post-entry.
P5152: 11586: bool MtNearestTpTarget(const int barShift, const ENUM_SRJ_DIR dir,
P5153: 11587:                        const double currentPrice, double &tpTargetOut)
P5154: 11588:   {
P5155: 11589:    double best = 0.0;
P5156: 11590:    bool   haveBest = false;
P5157: 11591:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5b/IE5c/IE8] election-day key + session names
P5158: 11592:    string uj_dk = UjDayKey(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift));
P5159: 11593:    const string uj_sname[18] = { "PDH", "PDL", "ASH", "ASL", "LOH", "LOL",
P5160: 11594:                                  "NYH", "NYL", "PMH", "PML",
P5161: 11595:                                  "YASH", "YASL", "YLOH", "YLOL",
P5162: 11596:                                  "YNYH", "YNYL", "YPMH", "YPML" };
P5163: 11597:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] one-shot closed-session touch detection.
P5164: 11598:    //--- Levels come from already-closed sessions only (PD NY pair = yesterday
P5165: 11599:    //--- closed; walker pool = strictly older), so the bar-that-closes-the-session
P5166: 11600:    //--- ineligibility holds by construction; lifetime-bound to [fill, now].
P5167: 11601:    double uj_oldTP = g_mtrade.tpRef;
P5168: 11602:    string uj_bk8 = TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5169: 11603:    bool uj_firedNow = false;
P5170: 11604:    if(g_mtrade.active && g_mtrade.state == MT_MANAGING && !g_mtrade.uj_touchDone)
P5171: 11605:      {
P5172: 11606:       datetime uj_bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5173: 11607:       if(uj_bt > 0 && g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0 && uj_bt > g_mtrade.fillBarTime)
P5174: 11608:         {
P5175: 11609:          double uj_h = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5176: 11610:          double uj_l = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5177: 11611:          double uj_o = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5178: 11612:          double uj_c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5179: 11613:          double uj_lv[10]; int uj_ln = 0;
P5180: 11614:          double uj_t = 0.0;
P5181: 11615:          if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_PD_NY_HIGH, uj_t, barShift) && uj_t != EMPTY_VALUE && uj_t > 0.0 && uj_ln < 10) { uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_t; uj_ln++; }
P5182: 11616:          if(ReadFlow(FL_BUF_PD_NY_LOW, uj_t, barShift) && uj_t != EMPTY_VALUE && uj_t > 0.0 && uj_ln < 10) { uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_t; uj_ln++; }
P5183: 11617:          string uj_todayK = UjDayKey(uj_bt);
P5184: 11618:          for(int uj_pi = 0; uj_pi < ArraySize(uj_pool) && uj_ln < 10; uj_pi++)
P5185: 11619:            {
P5186: 11620:             if(uj_pool[uj_pi].dayKey >= uj_todayK) continue;
P5187: 11621:             double uj_pv = uj_pool[uj_pi].value;
P5188: 11622:             if(uj_pv == EMPTY_VALUE || uj_pv <= 0.0) continue;
P5189: 11623:             uj_lv[uj_ln] = uj_pv; uj_ln++;
P5190: 11624:            }
P5191: 11625:          for(int uj_li = 0; uj_li < uj_ln; uj_li++)
P5192: 11626:            {
P5193: 11627:             double uj_L = uj_lv[uj_li];
P5194: 11628:             bool uj_inR = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG) ? (uj_L > g_mtrade.entryPrice) : (uj_L < g_mtrade.entryPrice);
P5195: 11629:             if(!uj_inR) continue;
P5196: 11630:             if(!(uj_L >= uj_l && uj_L <= uj_h)) continue;
P5197: 11631:             double uj_lo = MathMin(uj_o, uj_c), uj_hi = MathMax(uj_o, uj_c);
P5198: 11632:             string uj_ty = ((uj_L >= uj_lo && uj_L <= uj_hi) ? "PRICE" : "WICK");
P5199: 11633:             g_mtrade.uj_touchDone = true; g_mtrade.uj_touchLevel = uj_L;
P5200: 11634:             g_mtrade.uj_touchType = uj_ty; g_mtrade.uj_touchBarTime = uj_bt;
P5201: 11635:             PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJTOUCH level=%s type=%s event_bar_key=%s admit_bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d", DoubleToString(uj_L, _Digits), uj_ty, uj_bk8, TimeToString(g_mtrade.uj_admitBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq);
P5202: 11636:             uj_firedNow = true; break;
P5203: 11637:            }
P5204: 11638:         }
P5205: 11639:      }
P5206: 11640:    //--- [S1-TP-PROMOTION-001] live promotion: prev-day session H/L join the
P5207: 11641:    //--- candidate walk (indices 10..17 -> swept bits 14..21, unset this stage).
P5208: 11642:    const int sessbufs[18] = { FL_BUF_PDAY_HIGH, FL_BUF_PDAY_LOW,
P5209: 11643:                               FL_BUF_ASIA_HIGH, FL_BUF_ASIA_LOW,
P5210: 11644:                               FL_BUF_LONDON_HIGH, FL_BUF_LONDON_LOW,
P5211: 11645:                               FL_BUF_NY_HIGH, FL_BUF_NY_LOW,
P5212: 11646:                               FL_BUF_PM_HIGH, FL_BUF_PM_LOW,
P5213: 11647:                               FL_BUF_PD_ASIA_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_ASIA_LOW,
P5214: 11648:                               FL_BUF_PD_LONDON_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_LONDON_LOW,
P5215: 11649:                               FL_BUF_PD_NY_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_NY_LOW,
P5216: 11650:                               FL_BUF_PD_PM_HIGH, FL_BUF_PD_PM_LOW };
P5217: 11651:    double s39_mask;
P5218: 11652:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_SWEPT_MASK, s39_mask, barShift)) s39_mask = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5219: 11653:    for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(sessbufs); i++)
P5220: 11654:      {
P5221: 11655:       double v;
P5222: 11656:       if(ReadFlow(sessbufs[i], v, barShift) && !TpSessionLevelFiltered(i, s39_mask))
P5223: 11657:          TpTargetUpdateBest(v, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest, uj_sname[i], uj_dk, -1);
P5224: 11658:      }
P5225: 11659:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5b] managed-side pool loop (same cache + entry;
P5226: 11660:    //--- tie session > pool > POI; makes always-exit-nearest any-age-true).
P5227: 11661:    if(SrjUjPoolConsumable(uj_dk))
P5228: 11662:      {
P5229: 11663:       for(int uji = 0; uji < ArraySize(uj_pool); uji++)
P5230: 11664:          TpTargetUpdateBest(uj_pool[uji].value, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest,
P5231: 11665:                             uj_pool[uji].source, uj_pool[uji].dayKey, uj_pool[uji].poolGen);
P5232: 11666:      }
P5233: 11667:    int anchorRank = (g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0)
P5234: 11668:                     ? g_authorityRank[g_mtrade.anchorLine] : INT_MAX;
P5235: 11669:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P5236: 11670:      {
P5237: 11671:        if(k == g_mtrade.anchorLine || (g_authorityRank[k] / 2) > (anchorRank / 2))
P5238: 11672:           continue;
P5239: 11673:    if(!UjPoiTargetValid(k, g_mtrade.anchorLine))
P5240: 11674:      { if(InpDebugLog) PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOISKIP bar=%s line=%s anchor=%s", TimeToString(iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), g_lineCode[k], (g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0 ? g_lineCode[g_mtrade.anchorLine] : "none")); continue; }
P5241: 11675:        double v;
P5242: 11676:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, v, barShift)) continue;
P5243: 11677:       TpTargetUpdateBest(v, dir, currentPrice, best, haveBest, g_lineCode[k], uj_dk, -1);
P5244: 11678:      }
P5245: 11679:    if(!haveBest)
P5246: 11680:      {
P5247: 11681:       //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] re-election record: no winner leaves TP
P5248: 11682:       //--- unchanged (explicit no-winner record, one-shot per trade).
P5249: 11683:       if(uj_firedNow)
P5250: 11684:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJREELECT bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d oldTP=%s newTP=UNCHANGED winner=NONE src=retarget-pool", uj_bk8, g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq, DoubleToString(uj_oldTP, _Digits));
P5251: 11685:       return false;
P5252: 11686:      }
P5253: 11687:    tpTargetOut = best;
P5254: 11688:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE8] re-election record: election output at the TP
P5255: 11689:    //--- assignment site (one-shot per trade).
P5256: 11690:    if(uj_firedNow)
P5257: 11691:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJREELECT bar_key=%s trade_seq=%I64d oldTP=%s newTP=%s winner=%s src=retarget-pool", uj_bk8, g_mtrade.uj_tradeSeq, DoubleToString(uj_oldTP, _Digits), DoubleToString(tpTargetOut, _Digits), uj_winnerSource);
P5258: 11692:    return true;
P5259: 11693:   }
P5260: 11694: 
P5261: 11695: //--- [P-SLDEF-2 E25] MTLIFE: one line per managed record at close (rows ==
P5262: 11696: //--- MTEXIT rows exactly; print-only, MTEXIT untouched). openBar = fill
P5263: 11697: //--- candle open ("-" if never filled); verdict/exit from the record.
P5264: 11698: //--- Boundary booleans are lifetime-overlap tests over [fill, exit): news =
P5265: 11699: //--- open when an in-window windowStart falls inside; day/week = open at the
P5266: 11700: //--- census's own 16:55-ET / Friday-17:00-ET marks (the same g_news arrays
P5267: 11701: //--- the edge-triggered census uses). Halt caveat: SrjNewsFinalize runs at
P5268: 11702: //--- end of run, so halt-exclusion is exact iff no in-window row halts
P5269: 11703: //--- (BLACKOUT_HALT count gates it; 0 on every baseline to date).
P5270: 11704: //--- Unevaluable (unfilled/uninit) prints -1.
P5271: 11705: void MtLifeEmit()
P5272: 11706:    {
P5273: 11707:     string mtlOpen = (g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0) ? TimeToString(g_mtrade.fillBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES) : "-";
P5274: 11708:     string mtlClose = (g_mtrade.exitBarTime > 0) ? TimeToString(g_mtrade.exitBarTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES) : "-";
P5275: 11709:     int mtlNews = -1, mtlDay = -1, mtlWeek = -1;
P5276: 11710:     if(g_news_init && g_mtrade.fillBarTime > 0 && g_mtrade.exitBarTime > 0)
P5277: 11711:       {
P5278: 11712:        mtlNews = 0; mtlDay = 0; mtlWeek = 0;
P5279: 11713:        for(int mtlI = 0; mtlI < SRJ_NEWS_ROWS; mtlI++)
P5280: 11714:           if(g_news_inWin[mtlI] == 1 && g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_winS[mtlI] && g_news_winS[mtlI] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlNews = 1; break; }
P5281: 11715:        for(int mtlD = 0; mtlD < g_news_dayN; mtlD++)
P5282: 11716:           if(g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_dayMarks[mtlD] && g_news_dayMarks[mtlD] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlDay = 1; break; }
P5283: 11717:        for(int mtlF = 0; mtlF < g_news_friN; mtlF++)
P5284: 11718:           if(g_mtrade.fillBarTime <= g_news_friMarks[mtlF] && g_news_friMarks[mtlF] < g_mtrade.exitBarTime) { mtlWeek = 1; break; }
P5285: 11719:       }
P5286: 11720:     string mtlLine = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTLIFE fields=11 openBar=%s dir=%s entry=%s sl=%s tp=%s verdict=%s closeBar=%s closePx=%s openAtNewsStart=%d openAtDayClose=%d openAtWeekClose=%d",
P5287: 11721:               mtlOpen, DirName(g_mtrade.dir),
P5288: 11722:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.entryPrice, _Digits),
P5289: 11723:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.slRef, _Digits),
P5290: 11724:               DoubleToString(g_mtrade.tpRef, _Digits),
P5291: 11725:               MtExitName(g_mtrade.exitReason),
P5292: 11726:               mtlClose, DoubleToString(g_mtrade.exitPrice, _Digits),
P5293: 11727:               mtlNews, mtlDay, mtlWeek);
P5294: 11728:     LwAudit("MTLIFE", mtlLine);
P5295: 11729:     Print(mtlLine);
P5296: 11730:    }
P5297: 11731: 
P5298: 11732: //--- [P-SLDEF-3 E29] MTFLIP: one line per HTF_FLIP verdict evaluation
P5299: 11733: //--- (print-only; the exit below is untouched). openBar = fill-bar shift
P5300: 11734: //--- (-1 unevaluable); biasBefore = anti-leg count on the previous bar
P5301: 11735: //--- (-1 unreadable); flipSourceBar = the eval bar when the flip is new,
P5302: 11736: //--- else the previous bar (first KNOWN flipped bar — a lower bound on the

P5350: 11834: // Spec section 4 site 3: the exit, evaluated at the NEXT candle's open. Called once
P5351: 11835: // per closed bar from OnTick AFTER the entry pipeline (section 7's separation: this
P5352: 11836: // function never touches the entry pipeline or any working-set field). Every verdict
P5353: 11837: // is logged (instrumentation-first, section 4's mitigation); an actual exit also
P5354: 11838: // emits the EXIT alert (ALERT-ONLY preserved - never an order). Same-bar priority
P5355: 11839: // when several tests fire together: SL, then TP_TOUCH, then POI_BODY_BREAK, then
P5356: 11840: // HTF_FLIP (when re-enabled; beats DAY_CLOSE on shared bars), then DAY_CLOSE
P5357: 11841: // (universal scope: every managed trade) (the conservative stop-first standard; MTEXIT/MTLIFE record terminal
P5358: 11842: // exit reasons so the operator can re-judge any instance).
P5359: ```
P5360: ```mql5
P5361: 11851:    double o = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);

P5363: 11853:    double l = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5364: 11854:    double c = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift);
P5365: 11855:    //--- the NEXT candle's open = the evaluation instant's price (section 4);
P5366: 11856:    //--- fail-soft to the evaluated bar's close if the next open cannot be read.
P5367: 11857:    double nextOpenPx = (barShift >= 1) ? iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, barShift - 1) : 0.0;
P5368: 11858:    if(nextOpenPx <= 0.0) nextOpenPx = c;
P5369: 11859:    double bodyLo = MathMin(o, nextOpenPx);

P5371: 11861:    double EPS = 0.001 * _Point;   // the T161K float guard, threshold-free semantics
P5372: 11862: 
P5373: 11863:    //--- PENDING_FILL (section 5.5): fill on the first touch of the entry level.
P5374: 11864:    //--- Under the next-open entry the fill bar's own open IS the entry, so this
P5375: 11865:    //--- fills at the first evaluation; the branch keeps the lifecycle complete.
P5376: 11866:    if(g_mtrade.state == MT_PENDING_FILL)
P5377: 11867:      {
P5378: 11868:       bool touched = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG) ? (l <= g_mtrade.entryPrice + EPS)
P5379: 11869:                                                 : (h >= g_mtrade.entryPrice - EPS);
P5380: 11870:       if(!touched)
P5381: 11871:         {
P5382: 11872:          //--- not filled yet: cancel on a bias flip (the three-flag conjunction is
P5383: 11873:          //--- the same event per sections 3.4/5.5)
P5384: 11874:          double ltfBias;

P5388: 11878:             if((int)MathRound(ltfBias) != want)
P5389: 11879:               {
P5390: 11880:                g_mtrade.state      = MT_CLOSED;
P5391: 11881:                g_mtrade.exitReason = MT_EXIT_CANCEL_BIAS;
P5392: 11882:                g_mtrade.exitBarTime = barTime;
P5393: 11883:                g_mtrade.exitPrice   = nextOpenPx;
P5394: 11884:                 if(InpDebugLog)

P5397: 11887:                                TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
P5398: 11888:                    MtLifeEmit();
P5399: 11889:                   }
P5400: 11890:               }

P5403: 11893:         }

P5425: **EX-5. Retarget, model touch, day-close verdict and verdict print - EA 11843-11850 (already carried at 10.5) then EA 11939-12025.** The 11939-12025 span carries the retarget block, the model touch, the day-close gate, and the `EXITVERDICT` print that 15.3 analyses.
P5426:
P5427: ```mql5
P5428: 11939: 
P5429: 11940:    //--- (c) the body-close exit: PRICE's BODY close through a BEHIND trigger line
P5430: 11941:    //--- (body = open -> next open, the T161K convention; "it must be body" - Q5).
P5431: 11942:    //--- A line's own gap/move alone never exits (Q3; section 1.3). Side is per bar
P5432: 11943:    //--- (section 5.2): a trigger line whose CURRENT value sits ahead of the trade
P5433: 11944:    //--- is a touch-target, not a body-close trigger. The census logs ALL twelve
P5434: 11945:    //--- lines per bar so every MT_EXIT_SCOPE variant is measurable from one run.
P5435: 11946:    for(int k = 0; k < POI_NLINES; k++)
P5436: 11947:      {
P5437: 11948:        double L;
P5438: 11949:        if(!ReadBuf1(g_hPoi, k, L, barShift)) continue;
P5439: 11950:        if(L == EMPTY_VALUE || L <= 0.0) continue;
P5440: 11951:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1 E14] same N1 body counter at the exit site. Grounding:
P5441: 11952:        //--- break needs bodyLo < L-EPS (LONG) / bodyHi > L+EPS (SHORT), both
P5442: 11953:        //--- strict: exact equality never breaks.
P5443: 11954:        if(bodyLo == L || bodyHi == L) g_n1_poiEqBody++;
P5444: 11955:        bool behind = (g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG)  ? (L < nextOpenPx)
P5445: 11956:                                                  : (L > nextOpenPx);
P5446: 11957:       bool through = false;
P5447: 11958:       if(behind)
P5448: 11959:         {
P5449: 11960:          if(g_mtrade.dir == DIR_LONG)  through = (bodyLo < L - EPS);
P5450: 11961:          else                          through = (bodyHi > L + EPS);
P5451: 11962:         }
P5452: 11963:        bool isTrigger = MtIsBreakTrigger(k);
P5453: 11964:        //--- [P-SLDEF-1b E19] exit-site pairing: the line verdict is known here.
P5454: 11965:        //--- Strictness says equality never sets `through`, so every paired
P5455: 11966:        //--- instance is expected ok (survived); a BREAK coincidence reports inv.
P5456: 11967:        if(bodyLo == L || bodyHi == L)
P5457: 11968:          { if(isTrigger && behind && through) g_n1_exitBodyInv++; else g_n1_exitBodySurv++; }
P5458: 11969:        if(InpDebugLog)

P5467: 11978:                      DoubleToString(bodyHi, _Digits),

P5469: 11980:       //--- [P-EXITRANK-6] anchor-rank gate (his 2026-09-23 rule: same-line cross never exits; only HIGHER-authority breaks exit; lower number = higher authority; amends charter 9.1(2) same-line case, supersedes E3).
P5470: 11981:       if(isTrigger && behind && through && !vBREAK && g_mtrade.anchorLine >= 0 && g_mtrade.anchorLine < POI_NLINES && g_authorityRank[k] < g_authorityRank[g_mtrade.anchorLine])
P5471: 11982:         {

P5473: 11984:          breakLineVal  = L;

P5475: 11986:         }
P5476: 11987:      }

P5517: **EX-12. Broker-close helper and its mode guard - EA 11786-11814.** Spliced because both Sonnet C8 and GLM C9 ask for the exact close predicate. The guard is one line: EA 11795 reads `if(InpMode != MODE_EXECUTE || MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) == 0)`, printing SKIP-NO-SEND and returning -1. There is no demo term and no live-close path in the saved EA.
P5518:
P5519: ```mql5
P5520: 11786: //--- to a distant SL/TP fill (X1: verdict 1.16439 vs stop fill 1.16510 at 17:00;
P5521: 11787: //--- X2: verdict 1.16093 vs target fill 1.16302 on 9/7). This helper closes the
P5522: 11788: //--- broker side for exactly those two legs, at the verdict bar. Identity (v11
P5523: 11789: //--- Luna B plus Astra P2/P3): entryPid resolves the live ticket via the
P5524: 11790: //--- sole resolver (pid-filtered scan, never stored-ticket trust); the close
P5525: 11791: //--- deal must carry the same pid with exit classification, then flatness;
P5526: 11792: //--- returns -1 policy no-send, 0 fail (NOTHING, refusal, or identity break), 1 sent-ok.
P5527: 11793: int MtCloseBrokerPosition(const string leg, const double refPx, const datetime barTime)
P5528: 11794:   {
P5529: 11795:    if(InpMode != MODE_EXECUTE || MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) == 0)
P5530: 11796:      {
P5531: 11797:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTCLOSE bar=%s leg=%s ticket=%I64u pid=%I64d ref=%s action=SKIP-NO-SEND " +
P5532: 11798:                   "mode=%d tester=%d (live stays alerts-only; ticket/pid are latched diagnostics, never close trust)",
P5533: 11799:                   TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), leg, g_mtrade.ticket, g_mtrade.entryPid,
P5534: 11800:                   DoubleToString(refPx, _Digits), (int)InpMode, (int)MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER));
P5535: 11801:       return -1;
P5536: 11802:      }
P5537: 11803:    long entryPid = g_mtrade.entryPid;
P5538: 11804:    ulong ticket = MtPidToTicket(entryPid);
P5539: 11805:    long pmagic = 0;
P5540: 11806:    if(ticket == 0 || !PositionSelectByTicket(ticket))
P5541: 11807:      {
P5542: 11808:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] MTCLOSE bar=%s leg=%s ticket=%I64u pid=%I64d ref=%s action=NOTHING-TO-CLOSE",
P5543: 11809:                   TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), leg, ticket, entryPid,
P5544: 11810:                   DoubleToString(refPx, _Digits));
P5545: 11811:       return 0;
P5546: 11812:      }
P5547: 11813:    pmagic = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC);
P5548: 11814:    g_trade.SetExpertMagicNumber((ulong)pmagic);
P5549: ```

P5563: ```mql5
P5564: 12070: 
P5565: 12071: //====================== [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8] UJ closure implementation ============
P5566: 12072: //--- print helper: EMPTY-safe double
P5567: 12073: string UjDbl(double v)
P5568: 12074:   {
P5569: 12075:    if(v == EMPTY_VALUE) return "EMPTY";
P5570: 12076:    return DoubleToString(v, 1);
P5571: 12077:   }
P5572: 12078: //--- day key YYYY.MM.DD at broker midnight (F327-328 alignment, EA-side)
P5573: 12079: string UjDayKey(datetime t)
P5574: 12080:   {
P5575: 12081:    if(t <= 0) return "";
P5576: 12082:    return TimeToString(StringToTime(TimeToString(t, TIME_DATE)), TIME_DATE);
P5577: 12083:   }
P5578: 12084: //--- whole-day difference bar-day minus key-day (-1 when unkeyed)
P5579: 12085: int UjDayDiff(datetime barT, string dayKey)
P5580: 12086:   {
P5581: 12087:    if(barT <= 0 || dayKey == "") return -1;
P5582: 12088:    return (int)((StringToTime(TimeToString(barT, TIME_DATE)) - StringToTime(dayKey)) / 86400);
P5583: 12089:   }
P5584: 12090: //--- shared 1R assertion (single print schema; callers abort on false)
P5585: 12091: bool SrjUjAssert1R(double entry, double sl, double tp, string barKey, string src,
P5586: 12092:                    double &riskOut, double &rewardOut, double &ROut)
P5587: 12093:   {
P5588: 12094:    int d = 0;
P5589: 12095:    if(tp > entry && sl < entry) d = 1;
P5590: 12096:    else if(tp < entry && sl > entry) d = -1;
P5591: 12097:    double risk = -1.0, reward = -1.0, R = -1.0;
P5592: 12098:    bool ok = false;
P5593: 12099:    if(d != 0)
P5594: 12100:      {
P5595: 12101:       risk = (d == 1) ? (entry - sl) : (sl - entry);
P5596: 12102:       reward = (d == 1) ? (tp - entry) : (entry - tp);
P5597: 12103:       R = (risk > 0.0) ? (reward / risk) : -1.0;
P5598: 12104:       ok = (risk > 0.0 && reward > 0.0 && reward >= risk);
P5599: 12105:      }
P5600: 12106:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJ1R bar=%s src=%s entry=%s sl=%s tp=%s risk=%s reward=%s R=%.2f verdict=%s",
P5601: 12107:                barKey, src, DoubleToString(entry, _Digits), DoubleToString(sl, _Digits),
P5602: 12108:                DoubleToString(tp, _Digits), DoubleToString(risk, _Digits),
P5603: 12109:                DoubleToString(reward, _Digits), R, (ok ? "PASS" : "FAIL"));
P5604: 12110:    riskOut = risk; rewardOut = reward; ROut = R;
P5605: 12111:    return ok;
P5606: 12112:   }
P5607: 12113: //--- pool consumability: READY for the election day (SHORT/EMPTY/FAILED/stale: no-consume)
P5608: 12114: bool SrjUjPoolConsumable(string dayKey)
P5609: 12115:   {
P5610: 12116:    return (uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY && dayKey != "" && uj_poolDayKey == dayKey);
P5611: 12117:   }
P5612: 12118: //--- history walker: builds temp pool over [2026.04.29, today]
P5613: 12119: int SrjHistPoolBuild(SUjPoolRec &out[], string &achStart, int &dayCnt, int &famRead, int &unavail, int &emptyValid)
P5614: 12120:   {
P5615: 12121:    ArrayResize(out, 0);
P5616: 12122:    achStart = ""; dayCnt = 0; famRead = 0; unavail = 0; emptyValid = 0;
P5617: 12123:    datetime reqD = StringToTime("2026.04.29");
P5618: 12124:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5619: 12125:    if(nowB <= 0 || reqD <= 0) return UJ_POOL_FAILED;
P5620: 12126:    datetime todayD = StringToTime(TimeToString(nowB, TIME_DATE));
P5621: 12127:    datetime maxOrigin = todayD - 2 * 86400;
P5622: 12128:    string sessName[4] = { "ASIA", "LONDON", "NY", "PM" };
P5623: 12129:    bool truncated = false;
P5624: 12130:    for(datetime D = reqD; D <= todayD; D += 86400)
P5625: 12131:      {
P5626: 12132:       datetime refT = D + 43200;
P5627: 12133:       int sh = iBarShift(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, refT, false);
P5628: 12134:       if(sh < 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5629: 12135:       datetime bt = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, sh);
P5630: 12136:       if(bt <= 0) { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5631: 12137:       if(StringToTime(TimeToString(bt, TIME_DATE)) != D) continue;
P5632: 12138:       dayCnt++;
P5633: 12139:       if(achStart == "") achStart = TimeToString(D, TIME_DATE);
P5634: 12140:       bool dayEmpty = true;
P5635: 12141:       for(int s = 0; s < 4; s++)
P5636: 12142:         {
P5637: 12143:          double vLH = 0.0, vLL = 0.0, vPH = 0.0, vPL = 0.0;
P5638: 12144:          if(!ReadFlow(10 + s * 2, vLH, sh) || !ReadFlow(11 + s * 2, vLL, sh) ||
P5639: 12145:             !ReadFlow(40 + s * 2, vPH, sh) || !ReadFlow(41 + s * 2, vPL, sh))
P5640: 12146:            { truncated = true; unavail++; break; }
P5641: 12147:          famRead += 4;
P5642: 12148:          double lv[4]; datetime ov[4]; string sv[4];
P5643: 12149:          lv[0] = vLH; ov[0] = D; sv[0] = "LIVE";
P5644: 12150:          lv[1] = vLL; ov[1] = D; sv[1] = "LIVE";
P5645: 12151:          lv[2] = vPH; ov[2] = D - 86400; sv[2] = "PD";
P5646: 12152:          lv[3] = vPL; ov[3] = D - 86400; sv[3] = "PD";
P5647: 12153:          for(int r = 0; r < 4; r++)
P5648: 12154:            {
P5649: 12155:             if(lv[r] == EMPTY_VALUE || lv[r] <= 0.0) continue;
P5650: 12156:             if(ov[r] < reqD || ov[r] > maxOrigin) continue;
P5651: 12157:             int side = ((r == 0 || r == 2) ? 0 : 1);
P5652: 12158:             string okey = TimeToString(ov[r], TIME_DATE);
P5653: 12159:             bool dup = false;
P5654: 12160:             for(int e = 0; e < ArraySize(out); e++)
P5655: 12161:               { if(out[e].dayKey == okey && out[e].sess == sessName[s] && out[e].side == side) { dup = true; break; } }
P5656: 12162:             if(dup) continue;
P5657: 12163:             int n = ArraySize(out);
P5658: 12164:             ArrayResize(out, n + 1);
P5659: 12165:             out[n].dayKey = okey; out[n].sess = sessName[s]; out[n].side = side;
P5660: 12166:             out[n].value = lv[r]; out[n].source = sv[r];
P5661: 12167:             out[n].closure = ov[r] + 86399; out[n].poolGen = uj_poolGen + 1;
P5662: 12168:             dayEmpty = false;
P5663: 12169:            }
P5664: 12170:          if(truncated) break;
P5665: 12171:         }
P5666: 12172:       if(truncated) break;
P5667: 12173:       if(dayEmpty) emptyValid++;
P5668: 12174:      }
P5669: 12175:    if(truncated) return (ArraySize(out) > 0 ? UJ_POOL_SHORT : UJ_POOL_FAILED);
P5670: 12176:    return UJ_POOL_READY;
P5671: 12177:   }
P5672: 12178: //--- unconditional refresh in the new-bar path (day-keyed rollover rebuild)
P5673: 12179: void SrjUjPoolRefresh()
P5674: 12180:   {
P5675: 12181:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5676: 12182:    string todayK = UjDayKey(nowB);
P5677: 12183:    if(todayK != "" && todayK == uj_poolDayKey && uj_pubState == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5678: 12184:      {
P5679: 12185:       if(InpDebugLog)
P5680: 12186:          PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=no-rebuild",
P5681: 12187:                      uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, uj_dayCount, uj_famRead, uj_unavail,
P5682: 12188:                      uj_emptyValid, uj_pubState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5683: 12189:       return;
P5684: 12190:      }
P5685: 12191:    uj_attemptState = UJ_POOL_BUILDING; uj_attemptCount++;
P5686: 12192:    SUjPoolRec tmp[];
P5687: 12193:    string ach = ""; int dc = 0, fr = 0, un = 0, ev = 0;
P5688: 12194:    int st = SrjHistPoolBuild(tmp, ach, dc, fr, un, ev);
P5689: 12195:    uj_attemptState = st;
P5690: 12196:    uj_reqStart = "2026.04.29"; uj_achStart = ach;
P5691: 12197:    uj_dayCount = dc; uj_famRead = fr; uj_unavail = un; uj_emptyValid = ev;
P5692: 12198:    if(st == UJ_POOL_READY)
P5693: 12199:      {
P5694: 12200:       int n = ArraySize(tmp);
P5695: 12201:       ArrayResize(uj_pool, n);
P5696: 12202:       for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
P5697: 12203:         {
P5698: 12204:          uj_pool[i].dayKey = tmp[i].dayKey; uj_pool[i].sess = tmp[i].sess;
P5699: 12205:          uj_pool[i].side = tmp[i].side; uj_pool[i].value = tmp[i].value;
P5700: 12206:          uj_pool[i].source = tmp[i].source; uj_pool[i].closure = tmp[i].closure;
P5701: 12207:          uj_pool[i].poolGen = tmp[i].poolGen;
P5702: 12208:         }
P5703: 12209:       uj_poolDayKey = todayK; uj_pubState = UJ_POOL_READY; uj_poolGen++;
P5704: 12210:      }
P5705: 12211:    //--- SHORT/FAILED: published pool untouched (last-good; stale never consumed:
P5706: 12212:    //--- the consumability gate requires day match + READY).
P5707: 12213:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5708: 12214:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLCOV req=%s ach=%s days=%d famRead=%d unavail=%d emptyValid=%d state=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d cadence=rebuild",
P5709: 12215:                   uj_reqStart, ach, dc, fr, un, ev, st, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen);
P5710: 12216:    if(InpDebugLog)
P5711: 12217:       PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSTATE dayKey=%s publishedPoolState=%d refreshAttemptState=%d poolGen=%d",
P5712: 12218:                   uj_poolDayKey, uj_pubState, uj_attemptState, uj_poolGen);
P5713: 12219:   }
P5714: 12220: //--- run-level pool-service record (zero admissions print the schema, never vacuous)
P5715: 12221: void SrjUjPoolFinalize()
P5716: 12222:   {
P5717: 12223:    if(!InpDebugLog) return;
P5718: 12224:    datetime nowB = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5719: 12225:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPOOLSVC requestedStart=%s achievedStart=%s runEnd=%s dayCount=%d familyRead=%d unavailable=%d emptyValid=%d finalState=%d attempt=%d poolGen=%d admissions=%d",
P5720: 12226:                uj_reqStart, uj_achStart, UjDayKey(nowB), uj_dayCount, uj_famRead,
P5721: 12227:                uj_unavail, uj_emptyValid, uj_attemptState, uj_attemptCount, uj_poolGen, uj_admitCount);
P5722: 12228:   }
P5723: 12229: //--- single probe printer (pass bar-time join key; M15 row gated on M15-new-bar)
P5724: 12230: void SrjUjProbeTuple(int barShift, datetime barTime)
P5725: 12231:   {
P5726: 12232:    string bk = TimeToString(barTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5727: 12233:    double h4 = EMPTY_VALUE, h1 = EMPTY_VALUE, m15 = EMPTY_VALUE, ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5728: 12234:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_HIGH, h4, barShift)) h4 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5729: 12235:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_MID, h1, barShift)) h1 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5730: 12236:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_HTF_LOW, m15, barShift)) m15 = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5731: 12237:    if(!ReadFlow(FL_BUF_LTF_BIAS, ltf, barShift)) ltf = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5732: 12238:    //--- probe-side DIV classifier: verbatim firing-walk bound, full domain
P5733: 12239:    int maxWalk = Bars(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT) - 1;
P5734: 12240:    int readFail = 0, emptyV = 0, zeroV = 0, latestNZ = 0;
P5735: 12241:    string kind = "-";
P5736: 12242:    bool complete = true;
P5737: 12243:    for(int s = barShift; s <= maxWalk; s++)
P5738: 12244:      {
P5739: 12245:       double verdict = EMPTY_VALUE;
P5740: 12246:       if(!ReadBuf1(g_hCqd, CQD_BUF_DIVVERDICT, verdict, s)) { readFail++; complete = false; continue; }
P5741: 12247:       if(verdict == EMPTY_VALUE) { emptyV++; continue; }
P5742: 12248:       int v = (int)MathRound(verdict);
P5743: 12249:       if(v == 0) { zeroV++; continue; }
P5744: 12250:       if(latestNZ == 0) { latestNZ = v; kind = ((MathAbs(v) == 1) ? "regular" : "hidden"); }
P5745: 12251:      }
P5746: 12252:    bool aligned = ((g_dir == DIR_LONG && (latestNZ == 1 || latestNZ == 2)) ||
P5747: 12253:                    (g_dir == DIR_SHORT && (latestNZ == -1 || latestNZ == -2)));
P5748: 12254:    string cls = (!complete ? "INCOMPLETE" : (latestNZ == 0 ? "ABSENT" : (aligned ? "ALIGNED" : "OPPOSING")));
P5749: 12255:    PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJPROBE bar_key=%s h4=%s h1=%s m15=%s confirmedFeed=1 ltf=%s div=%s kind=%s readFail=%d empty=%d zero=%d complete=%d latestNZ=%d covReq=%s covAch=%s dayCount=%d ticktime=%s lag=chartTime-1bar",
P5750: 12256:                bk, UjDbl(h4), UjDbl(h1), UjDbl(m15), UjDbl(ltf), cls, kind,
P5751: 12257:                readFail, emptyV, zeroV, (complete ? 1 : 0), latestNZ,
P5752: 12258:                ((uj_reqStart == "") ? "-" : uj_reqStart), ((uj_achStart == "") ? "-" : uj_achStart),
P5753: 12259:                uj_dayCount, TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS));
P5754: 12260:    //--- IE10A: M15 buffer-row print fires only on M15-new-bar ticks
P5755: 12261:    static datetime uj_lastM15 = 0;
P5756: 12262:    datetime m15t = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 0);
P5757: 12263:    if(m15t <= 0)
P5758: 12264:      { if(InpDebugLog) PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15RF bar_key=%s", bk); }
P5759: 12265:    else if(uj_lastM15 == 0)
P5760: 12266:      { uj_lastM15 = m15t;
P5761: 12267:        PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15ROW bar_key=%s m15time=%s m15vote=%s", bk, TimeToString(m15t, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), UjDbl(m15)); }
P5762: 12268:    else if(m15t != uj_lastM15)
P5763: 12269:      { uj_lastM15 = m15t;
P5764: 12270:        PrintFormat("[SRJ-EA] UJM15ROW bar_key=%s m15time=%s m15vote=%s", bk, TimeToString(m15t, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), UjDbl(m15)); }
P5765: 12271:   }
P5766: 12272: 
P5767: 12273: //====================== OnTick =========================================
P5768: 12274: void OnTick()
P5769: 12275:   {
P5770: 12276:    static datetime s_lastBarTime = 0;
P5771: 12277:    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
P5772: 12278:    if(currentBarTime == s_lastBarTime) return;
P5773: 12279:    s_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
P5774: 12280:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE5] unconditional pool refresh in the new-bar path
P5775: 12281:    SrjUjPoolRefresh();
P5776: 12282:    LoadWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P5777: 12283:    //--- [P-UJIMPL-IMPL-1 v8 IE9/IE4] boundary order: memo-clear, probe-print,
P5778: 12284:    //--- then evaluation (non-load-bearing beyond this sentence).
P5779: 12285:    uj_memo_valid = false; uj_memo_barTime = 0; uj_memo_src = "";
P5780: 12286:    SrjUjProbeTuple(1, currentBarTime);
P5781: 12287:    EvaluateClosedBar(1, currentBarTime);
P5782: 12288:    StoreWorkingSet(1, currentBarTime);
P5783: 12289:    //--- [P-EXITMODEL] the section 4 site-3 exit phase: runs AFTER the entry pipeline
P5784: 12290:    //--- and AFTER the working-set store (it touches NO working-set field - section 7
P5785: 12291:    //--- separation). Evaluates the managed trade at the NEXT candle's open.
P5786: 12292:    EvaluateManagedTrade(1);
P5787: 12293:    //--- [P-NEWS-1 E22] blackout census hook, last: observes the settled state.
P5788: 12294:    SrjNewsInit();
P5789: 12295:    if(InpDebugLog && SHADOW_NEWS)
P5790: 12296:       SrjNewsOnBar(currentBarTime);
P5791: 12297:   }
P5792: 12298: //+------------------------------------------------------------------+
P5793: ```
P5794:
P5795: **EX-6. Abort and reset bodies - EA 6571-6634.** `ResetSequence` (EA 6571) and `GoAbort` (EA 6600), spliced whole because 15.5's abort-path argument rests on their bodies.
P5796:
P5797: ```mql5
P5798:  6571: void ResetSequence()
P5799:  6572:   {
P5800:  6573:    g_state          = ST_IDLE;
P5801:  6574:    g_dir            = DIR_NONE;
P5802:  6575:    SrjSideNote("ResetSequence", g_dir);
P5803:  6576:    g_regime         = REGIME_NONE;
P5804:  6577:    g_sessionAtEntry = SESSION_NONE;
P5805:  6578:    g_anchorLine     = -1;
P5806:  6579:    g_anchorPrice    = 0.0;
P5807:  6580:    g_anchorBarTime  = 0;
P5808:  6581:    g_divLatch       = false;
P5809:  6582:    g_touchSeen      = false;
P5810:  6583:    g_touchBarHi     = 0.0;
P5811:  6584:    g_touchBarLo     = 0.0;
P5812:  6585:    g_zoneHi         = 0.0;
P5813:  6586:    g_zoneLo         = 0.0;
P5814:  6587:    g_alertedArmed   = false;
P5815:  6588:    g_alertedSignal  = false;
P5816:  6589:    g_latchedEntry   = 0.0;
P5817:  6590:    g_latchedSl      = 0.0;
P5818:  6591:    g_latchedTp      = 0.0;
P5819:  6592:    g_latchedR       = 0.0;
P5820:  6593:    g_latchBarTime   = 0;
P5821:  6594:    g_confirmFromState = ST_IDLE;
P5822:  6595:    //--- [P-BUILD3 E5] no new working-set field: the re-bind assigns anchor,
P5823:  6596:    //--- price, time, zone, touch, state, latch + confirmFrom only — all are
P5824:  6597:    //--- existing members (fields 4/5/6, 11/12, 8/9/10, 0, 15-19, 20).
P5825:  6598:   }
P5826:  6599: 
P5827:  6600: void GoAbort(const string reason, ENUM_SRJ_STATE atState)
P5828:  6601:   {
P5829:  6602:    LogAbort(reason, atState);
P5830:  6603:    if(InpDebugLog && g_dir != DIR_NONE)
P5831:  6604:      {
P5832:  6605:       string a6rBT = TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES);
P5833:  6606:       string a6rLn = StringFormat("[SRJ-EA] A6REFUSED class=ABSENT_DECLINED bar=%s state=%s dir=%s predicate=%s",
P5834:  6607:                                   a6rBT, StateName(atState), DirName(g_dir), reason);
P5835:  6608:       A6Emit("REF" + a6rBT + reason + StateName(atState), a6rLn);
P5836:  6609:      }   //--- [A6-HOOK] (ii) refused decision row (candidate alive => born+rejected)
P5837:  6610:    //--- TASK 19c: count NO_REGIME aborts so the census can be read against
P5838:  6611:    //--- them directly. Measurement only.
P5839:  6612:    if(InpDebugLog && reason == ABORT_NO_REGIME) g_ea19_noRegimeAborts++;
P5840:  6613:    if(InpAlertStandDown && g_alertedArmed && !g_alertedSignal)
P5841:  6614:       EmitAlert("STAND-DOWN", "reason=" + reason, false);
P5842:  6615: 
P5843:  6616:    //--- TASK 15: shadow record. Must fire BEFORE ResetSequence()
P5844:  6617:    //--- clears g_dir and g_anchorLine. Read-only measurement.
P5845:  6618:    if(InpDebugLog &&
P5846:  6619:       (reason == ABORT_NO_REGIME || reason == ABORT_LTF_MISALIGN))
P5847:  6620:      {
P5848:  6621:       g_shadowActive = true;
P5849:  6622:       g_shadowDir    = g_dir;
P5850:  6623:       g_shadowLine   = g_anchorLine;
P5851:  6624:       g_shadowOpened = g_anchorBarTime;
P5852:  6625:       g_shadowSess   = g_sessionAtEntry;
P5853:  6626:       g_shadowFail   = reason;
P5854:  6627:       g_shadowBars   = 0;
P5855:  6628:      }
P5856:  6629: 
P5857:  6630:    ENUM_SRJ_STATE prev = g_state;
P5858:  6631:    g_state = ST_ABORT;
P5859:  6632:    LogState(prev, g_state);
P5860:  6633:    ResetSequence();
P5861:  6634:   }
P5862: ```

P5864: **EX-7. Session-position guard - EA 1754-1763.** The whole `IsSessionPositionOpen` body, which O6 ordering depends on.
P5865:
P5866: ```mql5
P5867:  1754: bool IsSessionPositionOpen(long magic)
P5868:  1755:   {
P5869:  1756:    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
P5870:  1757:      {
P5871:  1758:       ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
P5872:  1759:       if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == magic)
P5873:  1760:          return true;
P5874:  1761:      }
P5875:  1762:    return false;
P5876:  1763:   }
P5877: ```
